Mirror descent with constraints h_k≤M: We can find max sum via multiplicative updates:

Can we exploit LP dual to get continuous optimal efficiently without SciPy? Let's examine dual for fixed f:
Primal P: max 1^T b s.t. (A_f b)_k ≤1 (after scaling), b≥0.
Dual D: min 1^T y s.t. (A_f^T y)_j ≥1, y≥0.
Here y length 2n-1. Because f changes, M factor. Suppose M=1 scaling. The dual optimum = primal max Bsum. The condition A_f^T y ≥1. A_f^T y at j = ∑_{d=0}^{n-1} f_d y_{j+d}. So y is a nonnegative sequence such that every n-window weighted by f is at least1; minimize sum y. This is like covering. For uniform f=1/n, constraints ∑_{d=0}^{n-1} y_{j+d}/n ≥1 => sliding sum ≥n, min total y= n^2? Variables 2n-1, minimum? y constant n? Sum y=n(2n-1)~2n^2; objective? For M scaling? Not important.

If f is power law, y might be flat? The dual constraints are sliding weighted sums. For optimal f symmetric? Could be solved via greedy? Since weights f nonnegative, minimize total y to make weighted moving average ≥1. This is a linear program with totally unimodular? Matrix is banded 0-1 convolved with f (weights real). best = initial_best (with normalized sum=1)

   M = max(conv(best,best))
   if M <= 0: break
   A_scaled = A(best) / M
   res = linprog(...)
   if success:
       b = res.x
       Bsum = sum(b)
       if Bsum > best_sum? (which is 1) etc
       g = b / Bsum
       candidates = [g, mix best with g for t in [0.5,0.8,1]]
       for cand: score=eval; keep best if lower
       best = candidate with lowest score (maybe not LP solution if blend better)
       # maybe also normalize best to sum=1 each time (eval scale invariant; but LP assumes best sum=1). Normalize after updating.

Now design parametric optimization:
Function `score_fast(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 or not finite: return inf
an=a/s
if n small use np.convolve(an,an); else fft
maxb = conv.max()
return float(2*n*maxb)
```

Fast convolution:
```
def fast_conv(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if b is None: b=a
    na=a.size; nb=b.size
    if na==0 or nb==0: return np.empty(0)
    if na + nb <= 6000? use np.convolve (direct) maybe for exact speed.
       return np.convolve(a,b)
    # FFT
    nfft=1
    need=na+nb-1
    while nfft<need: nfft*=2
    za=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); zb=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(za*zb,nfft)[:need]
```
For a*a, b same; can square.
But `np.convolve` for 4096 direct 16M, fine. For 8192, 67M, FFT maybe faster. Threshold 3000. We'll choose `if max(na,nb) <= 2048: direct` else FFT. FFT may have small negative; clamp max? `c=np.maximum(c,0)`.
Score:
```
def sequence_score_fast(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12: return inf
    an=a/s
    c=fast_conv(an,an)
    mb=float(c.max())
    if not finite: return inf
    return 2*a.size*mb
```
Since sum=1 after normalization. Note if a all zero? inf.

Generate binomial:
```
def make_binomial_seq(n, alpha=0.5, taper_params=None):
    if n<=0: return []
    x=np.empty(n)
    v=1.0
    for i in range(n):
       x[i]=v
       if i+1<n:
          v *= (i+1-alpha)/(i+1)
    return x
```
For alpha maybe <=0? If alpha=0, ratio=1 constant; if alpha<0, ratio >? (i+1-alpha)>i+1 -> increasing; maybe not. We restrict.
Use vectorized recurrence? For n up to 5000 loop okay. Could use `np.cumprod` ratios:
```
ratios=(np.arange(n)+1-alpha)/np.arange(1,n+1); x=np.concatenate(([1], np.cumprod(ratios[1:])))`
```

def parametric_candidates(n, rng=None, budget=None):
    cands=[]
    base_q=make_binomial_seq(n,0.5)
    cands.append(normalize(base_q))
    # alpha sweep
    for alpha in [0.3,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.6,0.7]:
       x=make_binomial_seq(n,alpha)
       cands.append(normalize(x))
    # tapers on 0.5
    for t in [-0.5,-0.2,0.05,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       x=base_q*(1+t*np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1))
       if min(x)>0: cands.append(normalize(x))
    for t in [0.2,0.5,1,2,5]: sigma...
       bump = 1 + t*np.exp(-((x-0.9)/0.25)**2)
    for beta in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]:
       rev=base_q[::-1]
       x=(1-beta)*base_q + beta*rev  (if beta>1? need positive)
       if min>0: cands.append(normalize(x))
    # power 1/sqrt(i+1) (similar)
    for shift in [0.5,1,2,5]: x=1/np.sqrt(np.arange(n)+shift); cands.append(normalize)
    return cands
```

def local_polish(a, budget_s, rng, official_len_limit=8000):
   a=normalize(a.copy())
   best=a; sc=score_fast(a)
   deadline=time+...
   no_improve=0

   while time<deadline:
      cand=block_perturb(best, rng, sigma)

Block perturbation:
```
def block_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
   n=a.size; c=a.copy()
   width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//50), max(3,n//10)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
   c[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma))
   if rng.random()<0.3:
      width2=...; multiply by smooth? 
   if rng.random()<0.2:
      c = np.maximum(c, 0)
      c[start] ... endpoint tweaks
   c=np.clip(c,0,1000)
   return normalize(c)
```

Subgradient helper `subgrad_candidate(a, rng, eta, active_frac, num_steps)`:
Could be expensive. Implement with FFT for grad. Need function `subgrad_gradient(a, w)` top-level:
```
def subgrad_gradient(a, w):
    n=a.size
    # S sliding sum of w length n starting at i
    cum=np.empty(2*n+?); 
    cwn=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    S = cwn[n:n-1+n+?] - cwn[:n]
    # w length 2n-1, cwn len 2n. Need S_i = cwn[i+n]-cwn[i]
    S = cwn[n:n-1+n+? actually i=0..n-1 => cwn[i+n] indices n..2n-1? cwn length 2n, max i+n=2n-1, ok. S = cwn[n:2n] - cwn[0:n]
    # T via FFT cross-corr:
    nfft=1; while nfft < n + len(w) -1: nfft*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); fw=np.fft.rfft(w,nfft)
    T = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fw), nfft)[:n]
    return S*a + T
```
Let's test T: For a=[a0,a1], w length? n=2, w length3. Cross corr c[0]=a0 w0 + a1 w1; T0= a0w0 + a1 w1 yes. c[1]=a0w1 + a1w2; T1 sum w_{1+d} d=0,1 =a0w1+a1w2. Good.
Gradient of h_w: for active all? For w_k normalized. For n=2, h0=a0²,h1=2a0a1,h2=a1². If w_0=1, grad should [2a0,0]. Our S: S0=w0+w1? Wait sum w_i for d=0..1: S0=w0+w1, if only w0=1 S0=1; S1=w1+w2=0. T via FFT: T0=a0w0+a1w1=a0; T1=a1? if w? T1=0. grad=S*a+T=[a0+a0=2a0,0], good. For w1=1, S0=w1=1, S1=w1=1? For i=1: w1+w2=w1=1. T0=a1, T1=a0. grad=[a0+a1, a1+a0] good.
So formula works.

Subgradient update: We need choose active weights. Since objective is max over h, active weights can be any distribution over near-max. We can choose `w` proportional to `h_k^p` for high h or uniform. Because w length 2n-1.
```
def active_weights_from_conv(h, rng, alpha_active):
    maxh=h.max()
    # threshold = maxh - 0.05*maxh maybe
    active = h >= maxh - 0.1*maxh
    # Also add random: if rng.random()<0.5: idx=rng.choice(len(h), min(50,len(h)), replace=False); active[idx]=True
    w=np.zeros_like(h)
    w[active]=np.maximum(h[active],1e-12)**2  # emphasize
    if w.sum()==0: w[np.argmax(h)]=1
    return w/w.sum()
```
Step size: We can do multiple small steps. Since a normalized sum1, update multiplicatively:
```
def mirror_subgrad_step(a, w, eta):
    grad = subgrad_gradient(a,w)
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - eta*grad/(a+1e-300)
    # to keep stable, subtract max
    a2 = np.exp(loga - np.max(loga))
    a2 = np.clip(a2,0,1000)
    s=a2.sum()
    if s>0: a2/=s
    else: return a
    return a2
```
Could also use additive with clip on a. Multiplicative keeps positive.

def build_response_matrix(vals):
   n=len(vals); 
   # try diags
   if n <= 3000:
      try:
         diags = [np.full(n, vals[d]) for d in range(n)]
         offsets = np.arange(0, -n, -1, dtype=np.int32)
         A = sp.diags(diags, offsets=offsets, shape=(2*n-1, n), format='csc')
         return A
      except Exception as e: pass
   # COO block
   rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
   cols_base = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
   for d in range(n):
      rows_parts.append(cols_base + d)  # int32? max 2n < 2^31
      cols_parts.append(cols_base)
      data_parts.append(np.full(n, vals[d], dtype=np.float64))
   A=sp.coo_matrix((np.concatenate(data_parts), (np.concatenate(rows_parts), np.concatenate(cols_parts))), shape=(2*n-1,n)).tocsr()
   return A
```

Let's implement `build_response_matrix(vals)`:
```
def build_response_matrix(vals, n=None):
   if n is None: n=vals.size
   try:
      D = np.empty((n,n), dtype=np.float64)
      D[:,:] = vals
      offsets = np.arange(0, -n, -1, dtype=np.int32)
      A = sp.diags(D, offsets=offsets, shape=(2*n-1, n), format='csc')
      del D
      return A
   except Exception:
      # fallback coo block
      cols = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
      rows_all=[]; cols_all=[]; data_all=[]
      for d in range(n):
         rows_all.append(cols + d)
         cols_all.append(cols)
         data_all.append(np.full(n, vals[d], dtype=np.float64))
      A=sp.coo_matrix((np.concatenate(data_all), (np.concatenate(rows_all), np.concatenate(cols_all))), shape=(2*n-1,n)).tocsr()
      return A
```

def normalize_array(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15: return None
    return a / s
```
This returns normalized sum1. For LP parent we need sum1. Good.

Implement `active_weights`:
```
def make_active_weights(h, rng, target_sum=50.0):
   maxh=h.max(); minh=h.min()
   # Use top by quantile: top_frac=0.25
   thr = maxh - 0.25*(maxh - minh)
   active = np.where(h >= thr)[0]
   if len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(h))])
   # Add random 5 indices maybe
   for _ in range(5): active add rng.integers
   w=np.zeros_like(h); w[active]=1.0
   # weight by h[active]? maybe
   w[active] = np.maximum(h[active], h.max()*1e-6)
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0: w[0]=1; s=1
   w *= (target_sum/s)
   return w
```

Thus implement `diagonal_multiplicative_step(a, k, eta)`:
```
def diag_step(a,k,eta):
    n=a.size
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    if lo>hi: return a
    i=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
    d=k-i
    factor = a[i] + a[d]
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga[i] -= eta * factor
    a2 = np.exp(loga - np.max(loga))
    s=a2.sum()
    if s<=0: return a
    a2/=s
    return a2
```
But if a has zeros, log zeros -> log eps; exp small. Fine.
This reduces the selected diagonal? Let's check: new h_k =∑ (a_i e^{-η(a_i+a_d)})(a_d e^{-η(a_d+a_i)}) = e^{-2η a_i a? no factor same for pair? For pair (i,d), if i≠d, product multiplied by e^{-2η(a_i+a_d)}; self i=d multiplied e^{-2η(2a_i)}? Actually self factor e^{-η(2a_i)} squared=e^{-2η(2a_i)}. It reduces. Good.
Select k: current max or random top. Active tolerance: top 10% h values. We can select `k = active_pixels[rng.integers]`. Step size start 0.05, adapt if score improves.
This is extremely cheap, can run many steps. Let's design `diagonal_descent(a, budget_s, rng, max_steps)`:
```
a=normalize; best=a; sc=score_fast(a)
eta=0.05; no_improve=0
step=0
while time:
   h=fast_conv(a,a)
   maxh=h.max()
   # active weights: top fraction; choose random among active with low maybe
   active = np.where(h >= maxh - 0.05*(maxh - h.min()))[0]
   if len(active)<10: active = np.argsort(h)[-10:]
   k=int(active[rng.integers(len(active))])
   a2=diag_step(a,k,eta)
   sc2=sequence_score_fast(a2)
   if sc2 < sc - 1e-12:
       a=a2; best if lower; sc=sc2; no_improve=0; eta=min(0.2, eta*1.05)
   else:
       # try smaller eta
       no_improve +=1
       if no_improve>20:
           eta*=0.7; no_improve=0
           if eta < 1e-4: break/other method
       # optionally not update a
```
But if step worsens, we revert. This can get stuck but helps polish. Could accept slight worsen to escape? Keep best separate.
Use this after LP if time, maybe 60-120s. For n=2535, each step conv direct 6.4M ~0.005s + diag O(n). 1000 steps 5s. Good. Could be effective.

```
def run_lp_chain(parent, parent_score, rng, deadline, lp_n, best_seq, best_score):
    n=lp_n
    parent = normalize_array(parent)
    parent_score = sequence_score_fast(parent)
    best_seq,best_score = consider_array(parent, best_seq, best_score)
    no_imp=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       it += 1
       cur_time=...
       if n > 3000 or it > 200: break
       maxh = float(fast_conv(parent,parent).max())
       if maxh <= 0: break
       rhs = maxh * (1+1e-8)
       timeout = min(90.0, (deadline-cur_time)*0.7)
       if timeout < 10: break
       b = solve_response_lp(parent, rhs, timeout)
       if b is None:
           # maybe reduce n? break chain; local
           break
       b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
       b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1e6)
       bsum=float(b.sum())
       if bsum <= 1e-12: break
       # store best lp cand
       local_best = parent; local_best_sc = parent_score
       # candidate blends
       ts = [1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.1, 0.03]
       g = b/bsum
       for t in ts:
           cand = (1.0-t)*parent + t*g
           cand = normalize_array(cand)
           if cand is None: continue
           sc = sequence_score_fast(cand)
           if sc < local_best_sc:
               local_best = cand; local_best_sc = sc
           best_seq,best_score = consider_array(cand,best_seq,best_score)
       # also maybe g alone included t=1
       if local_best_sc < parent_score - 1e-10:
           parent=local_best; parent_score=local_best_sc; no_imp=0
           print
       else:
           no_imp +=1
           if no_imp >= 4:
              # Try targeted LP with slightly smaller rhs
              target_rhs = (parent_score * (1.0 - 0.001)) / (2.0*n)
              if target_rhs > 1e-12 and target_rhs < rhs:
                   b2=solve_response_lp(parent, target_rhs, min(60, ...))
                   evaluate blends similar; if improved no_imp=0 else:
                   # local polish parent 20s
                   parent2,parent_sc2 = diagonal_descent(parent, 20, rng)
                   if finite and parent_sc2 < parent_score+0.001:
                      parent=parent2; parent_score=parent_sc2; best_seq,best_score=consider_array(parent,...)
                   no_imp=0
       if it % 5 ==0: print
    return best_seq,best_score,parent,parent_score
```

```
def diagonal_descent(a, budget_s, rng, eta0=0.05):
   a=normalize_array(a); if None return original? 
   best=a; best_sc=score_fast(a); deadline=...
   eta=eta0; no=0; step=0
   while time<deadline:
      h=fast_conv(a,a); maxh=h.max()
      if not finite: break
      # active top 20%
      count=max(10, int(0.1*(2*a.size-1)))
      # choose active random: sample proportion to h?
      active = np.where(h >= maxh - 0.1*(maxh - h.min()))[0]
      if len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(h))])
      k=int(active[rng.integers(len(active))])
      a2=diag_multiplicative_step(a,k,eta)
      sc2=score_fast(a2)
      if sc2 < sc - 1e-12:
         a=a2; sc=sc2
         if sc2 < best_sc: best=a2.copy(); best_sc=sc2
         no=0
         if no<30: eta=min(0.2, eta*1.03)
      else:
         no +=1
         if no > 25:
            eta *= 0.75; no=0
            if eta < 1e-4: break
      step+=1
   return best,best_sc
```

`fast_conv`: Need avoid `np.fft` for small. Use `if max(na,nb) <= 2500: return np.convolve(a,b)`. For a,b normalized, length maybe. For n=2535, n<=2500? no, uses FFT for 2535. FFT first time might be okay. Could set threshold 3000 to use direct for 2535 (fast). Let's set 3000. `np.convolve` direct 6.4M, fine. For 4096 direct 16M, maybe still okay but many scores? Use FFT if >3000. So 2535 direct. Good.
```
def fast_conv(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if b is None: b=a
    na=a.size; nb=b.size
    if na==0 or nb==0: return np.empty(0,float)
    if max(na,nb) <= 3000:
       c=np.convolve(a,b)
    else:
       need=na+nb-1; nfft=1
       while nfft < need: nfft <<=1
       za=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); zb=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
       c=np.fft.irfft(za*zb, nfft)[:need]
       c=np.where(c>0,c,0.0) # remove tiny neg
    return c
```
If b is None and a same, could use square for FFT: `za*np.conj?` No, convolution a*a not autocorrelation; for FFT, convolution of a with a is `za*za` (not conj). Yes because same transform. Use `za*za`. Good. For b different, `za*zb`. In our use same. `irfft` may produce tiny negative; clamp.
Direct `np.convolve` for a*a also, no issue.

`sequence_score_fast`:
```
def sequence_score_fast(a):
   arr=normalize_array(a)
   if arr is None: return np.inf
   n=arr.size
   c=fast_conv(arr,arr)
   if c.size==0: return np.inf
   mb=float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(mb): return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*mb)
```
This normalizes; if arr sum1.

`official_score`:
```
def official_score(a):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,float)
       arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       if arr.size==0: return np.inf
       val=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
       if val is None: return np.inf
       return float(val)
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
```
This will call global. No import.

`consider_array`:
```
def consider_array(a,best_seq,best_score):
    if a is None: return best_seq,best_score
    arr=normalize_array(a)
    if arr is None: return best_seq,best_score
    sc=sequence_score_fast(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
       return arr,sc
    return best_seq,best_score
```
It normalizes again; okay.

       out[i]=v

```
def run_parametric_length(n, rng, best_seq, best_score, deadline):
    if n <=0: return
    # print
    xfrac = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    base = make_binomial(n,0.5)
    best_seq,best_score=consider_array(base,best_seq,best_score)
    # alphas
    for alpha in [0.6,0.7,0.8,1.0,1.2,0.4]:
       if time > deadline-3: break
       arr=make_binomial(n,alpha)
       best_seq,best_score=consider_array(arr,...)
    # powers 1/sqrt(i+shift)
    for shift in [0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,20.0]:
       arr=1.0/np.sqrt(np.arange(n)+shift)
       consider
    # gamma transform of base
    for gamma in [0.6,0.8,1.2,1.5,2.0]:
       arr=np.power(base, gamma)
       consider
    # reverse mixes
    rev=base[::-1]
    for beta in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0]:
       arr=base + beta*rev
       consider
    # bump additive
    for amp in [0.01,0.05,0.2,1.0,3.0]:
       for mu in [0.7,0.85,1.0]:
          for sig in [0.1,0.2,0.4]:
             if time > deadline-2: break
             gauss=np.exp(-(((xfrac-mu)/sig)**2))
             arr=base + amp*gauss
             consider
          ...
    # linear factors
    for t in [-0.5,-0.2,0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,3.0]:
       arr=base * (1.0 + t*xfrac)
       if np.min(arr) > -1e-9: consider
       arr=base * (1.0 - 0.5*xfrac + t*xfrac*xfrac?) maybe not
    # shifted power? base2 = (i+1)^-alpha * (n-i+1)^-beta? Could.
    return
```
Need handle breaks in nested loops efficiently. Use a helper flag? Since no nested function, use `too_late` boolean and break conditions. Simpler check at top of outer loops; nested might continue but not too many.

The number of bump candidates: ampf 5*mu3*sig3=45. okay. For many lengths 6 ->270.

`diag_multiplicative_step`:
```
def diag_multiplicative_step(a, k, eta):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n ==0 or k<0 or k>=2*n-1: return a
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    if lo>hi: return a
    idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1,dtype=int)
    d=k-idx
    factor=a[idx] + a[d]
    loga=np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga[idx] = loga[idx] - eta * factor
    # Renormalize using exp
    a2=np.exp(loga - np.max(loga))
    s=a2.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return a
    a2/=s
    return a2
```
If a has zeros, log -690, exp maybe zero but okay. Eta maybe large causing underflow; but max subtract. Good.

`diagonal_descent`:
```
def diagonal_descent(a, budget_s, rng, initial_eta=0.1):
   a=normalize_array(a)
   if a is None: return None,np.inf
   orig=a.copy()
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=sequence_score_fast(a); sc=best_sc
   deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
   eta=initial_eta
   no=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      h=fast_conv(a,a)
      if not finite: break
      maxh=float(h.max()); minh=float(h.min())
      # active top: h >= maxh - 0.1*(maxh-minh)
      if maxh > 0:
          thr = maxh - 0.1*(maxh - minh)
      else: thr=maxh
      active=np.where(h >= thr)[0]
      if active.size ==0:
          active=np.array([int(np.argmax(h))])
      # maybe cap active to 200? Random choose; if huge, choose random subset maybe to reduce bias
      if active.size > 200:
          active = active[rng.choice(active.size, 200, replace=False)]
      k=int(active[rng.integers(active.size)])
      a2=diag_multiplicative_step(a,k,eta)
      sc2=sequence_score_fast(a2)
      if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-12:
         a=a2; sc=sc2; no=0
         if sc2 < best_sc:
            best=a2.copy(); best_sc=sc2
         eta = min(0.3, eta*1.02)
      else:
         # accept small worsening sometimes
         if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc * (1.0+0.005) and rng.random() < 0.2:
             a=a2; sc=sc2; no=0
         else:
             no +=1
             if no > 30:
                 eta *= 0.75
                 no=0
                 if eta < 1e-5:
                    break
   return best,best_sc
```
This may be slow if sequence_score_fast normalizes and conv direct. Budget 20s. Good.

`random_block_perturb`:
```
def random_block_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if n<=1: return a.copy()
   c=a.copy()
   # choose 1-3 blocks
   nblocks = 1 + (rng.random()<0.3)
   for _ in range(nblocks):
      width = int(rng.integers(max(2, n//200), max(3, n//20)))
      width=min(width,n)
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      mult=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma))
      c[start:start+width] *= mult
      if rng.random()<0.2:
         mult2=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma))
         c[start:start+width] *= mult2
   # smooth? maybe convolve small kernel? skip
   c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
   c=np.clip(c,0,1000)
   return normalize_array(c)
```
`local_block_search`:
```
def local_block_search(a,budget_s,rng):
   a=normalize_array(a)
   if a is None: return None,inf
   best=a; sc=score; deadline; sigma=0.15; no=0
   while time<deadline:
      cand=random_block_perturb(best,rng,sigma)
      sc2=score(cand)
      if finite and sc2 < sc*(1+0.002) and (sc2 < sc or rng.random()<0.25):
          best=cand; sc=sc2; no=0
          if sc2 < best_sc...; sigma=min(0.3,sigma*1.05)
      else:
          no+=1
          if no>50: sigma*=0.8; no=0
          if sigma<0.01: break
   return best,best_sc
```

In `solve_response_lp`, if `vals.size` > 4000 return None to prevent memory. nLP 2535 okay.
```
def solve_response_lp(a, rhs, timeout_s=60.0):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    a=normalize_array(a)
    if a is None: return None
    n=a.size
    if n < 16 or n > 3500: return None
    rhs=float(rhs)
    if not finite or rhs <=0: return None
    vals=a / rhs
    if not finite all: return None
    A=build_response_matrix(vals)
    if A is None: return None
    c=-np.ones(n)
    b_ub=np.ones(2*n-1)
    bounds=(0.0, None) # maybe sequence? Use [ (0,None) ]*n? Safer: bounds=np.zeros? Let's use `bounds=(0.0, np.inf)`? In linprog, tuple of two floats applies to all variables. Use (0.0, np.inf).
    options={'time_limit': max(1.0, float(timeout_s))}
    try:
       res=opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=options)
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
          return None
    if res is None: return None
    x=getattr(res,'x',None)
    if x is None: return None
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if x.size != n: return None
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1e8)
    # If res failed and x all zero, maybe no.
    return x
```
`bounds=(0.0, np.inf)` works? According to docs, bounds can be (lb, ub) for all. Yes.

`run_lp_chain` details. It should only use `solve_response_lp` if scipy. It can run multiple LPs. It needs global best and local.
```
def run_lp_chain(parent, parent_score, rng, deadline, n, best_seq, best_score):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return ...
    a=normalize_array(parent); if None return
    parent_score=sequence_score_fast(a)
    best_seq,best_score=consider_array(a,...)
    no=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
       it +=1
       if parent is None: break
       h=fast_conv(parent,parent)
       cur_max=float(h.max())
       if not finite or cur_max <=0: break
       remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
       timeout = min(120.0, remaining*0.6)
       if timeout < 20: break
       rhs=cur_max*(1+1e-7)
       b=solve_response_lp(parent, rhs, timeout)
       if b is None:
           # maybe reduce timeout? break
           break
       b=np.clip(b,0,1e6)
       bsum=float(b.sum())
       if bsum <= 1e-12: break
       g=b/bsum
       local_best=parent; local_best_sc=parent_score
       # candidate list t
       for t in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.1,0.03]:
          cand = (1.0-t)*parent + t*g
          cand=normalize_array(cand)
          if cand is None: continue
          sc=sequence_score_fast(cand)
          if finite and sc < local_best_sc - 1e-12:
             local_best=cand; local_best_sc=sc
          best_seq,best_score=consider_array(cand,best_seq,best_score)
       # maybe if bsum very close 1 and local no improve;
       if local_best_sc < parent_score - 1e-10:
          parent=local_best; parent_score=local_best_sc; no=0
          print(f"LP iter {it}: improved parent to {parent_score:.8f}")
       else:
          no +=1
          print(f"LP iter {it}: no improvement (bsum={bsum:.4f})")
          if no >= 3:
             # try target rhs slightly smaller? Use if time
             target_sc = parent_score * (1 - 0.0015)
             target_rhs = target_sc/(2.0*n)
             if target_rhs > 1e-12 and target_rhs < rhs and remaining > 90:
                 b2=solve_response_lp(parent, target_rhs, min(60, remaining*0.4))
                 if b2 is not None:
                    b2sum=b2.sum()
                    if b2sum > 1e-12:
                       g2=b2/b2sum
                       for t in [1.0,0.5,0.2]:
                          cand=(1-t)*parent + t*g2
                          ... update local_best and global
                       if local_best_sc < parent_score:
                           parent=local_best; no=0
             if no >= 5:
                # local diagonal descent for 15s
                a2,sc2=diagonal_descent(parent, 15.0, rng)
                if a2 is not None and sc2 < parent_score:
                    parent=a2; parent_score=sc2; best_seq,best_score=consider_array(parent,...); no=0
                else:
                    # random block search
                    a2,sc2=local_block_search(parent, 10.0, rng)
                    if ... update if not too worse? maybe accept if sc2 < parent_score + 0.001 to escape
                    no=0
    return best_seq,best_score,parent,parent_score
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1800.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total - 10.0)
    best_seq = None; best_score = np.inf
    # height_sequence
    try:
       h = height_sequence_1  # NameError if not exist
       best_seq,best_score=consider_array(h,best_seq,best_score)
    except Exception:
       pass
    # parametric lengths list
    lengths = [2535]
    # if time > 300, add others? We can always loop with deadline; time checks.
    for N in [2535, 2048, 4096, 1024, 8192, 1536, 512]:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       # skip N if too large
       if N > 10000: continue
       run_parametric_length(N, rng, best_seq, best_score, deadline)
       print(f"After parametric len {N}: best={best_score:.6f}")
    # LP track
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
       # choose lp_n based on available best if length in [800,3500]
       lp_n = 2535
       # if best length is in 1024..4096 maybe use that? Use 2535 always.
       lp_parent = normalize_array(make_binomial(lp_n, 0.5))
       if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == lp_n:
           sc_best=sequence_score_fast(best_seq)
           if sc_best < sequence_score_fast(lp_parent):
               lp_parent=best_seq
       # prepolish parent 15s
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80:
           lp_parent, lp_score = diagonal_descent(lp_parent, 20.0, rng)
           best_seq,best_score=consider_array(lp_parent,best_seq,best_score)
       else:
           lp_score=sequence_score_fast(lp_parent)
       # first LP chain
       lp_deadline = min(deadline - 60, time.monotonic() + max(60, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.55))
       best_seq,best_score,lp_parent,lp_score = run_lp_chain(lp_parent, lp_score, rng, lp_deadline, lp_n, best_seq,best_score)
       # second chain n=1024 if time
       if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
          n2=1024
          parent2=normalize_array(make_binomial(n2,0.5))
          if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n2 and sequence_score_fast(best_seq)<sequence_score_fast(parent2): parent2=best_seq
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
             parent2,_=diagonal_descent(parent2,10.0,rng)
          dp2 = min(deadline - 30, time.monotonic()+max(30,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.4))
          best_seq,best_score,parent2,score2=run_lp_chain(parent2, score2 if defined, rng, dp2, n2, best_seq,best_score)
    # final local search
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
       # local diagonal up to 1/3 remaining, cap 180
       budget = min(180.0, max(5.0, remaining*0.4))
       a2,sc2=diagonal_descent(best_seq,budget,rng)
       if a2 is not None and sc2 < best_score:
           best_seq,best_score = a2,sc2
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
           budget2 = min(90.0, max(5.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.5))
           a2,sc2=local_block_search(best_seq,budget2,rng)
           if a2 is not None and sc2 < best_score:
               best_seq,best_score = a2,sc2
    # fallback
    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size ==0 or not np.isfinite(best_seq).all():
       best_seq = np.ones(64)
       best_score = sequence_score_fast(best_seq)
    # final cleanup
    arr=np.asarray(best_seq,float)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <=0: arr=np.ones(64)
    # Should we normalize? Evaluator scale invariant, but if sum very small maybe inf; keep sum enough. If normalized sum1, fine.
    if s >0 and s < 0.01:
       arr = arr / s  # sum1
    print(f"final length={arr.size} best fast score={best_score}")
    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

def run_lp_chain(...):
    n = int(lp_n)
    parent = normalize_array(parent)
    if parent is None: return ...
    parent_score = sequence_score_fast(parent)
    best_seq,best_score = consider_array(parent,...)
    no=0
    it=0
    max_iters = 200
    while it < max_iters:
       it +=1
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 25: break
       h = fast_conv(parent,parent)
       cur_max = float(h.max())
       if not np.isfinite(cur_max) or cur_max <= 0: break
       remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
       timeout = min(120.0, remaining*0.55)
       if timeout < 20: break
       # choose rhs
       rhs = cur_max * (1e-8 + 1.0)
       b = solve_response_lp(parent, rhs, timeout)
       if b is None:
          # retry with smaller n? just break
          break
       b = np.asarray(b,float); b=np.where(np.isfinite(b),b,0); b=np.clip(b,0,10)
       bsum=float(b.sum())
       if bsum <= 1e-12: break
       g = b / bsum
       local_best=parent; local_best_sc=parent_score
       for t in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.1,0.03]:
          cand=(1-t)*parent + t*g
          cand=normalize_array(cand)
          if cand is None: continue
          sc=sequence_score_fast(cand)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
             local_best=cand; local_best_sc=sc
          best_seq,best_score=consider_array(cand,best_seq,best_score)
       if local_best_sc < parent_score - 1e-10:
          parent=local_best; parent_score=local_best_sc; no=0
          if it % 2 ==0: print
       else:
          no += 1
          if no >= 2:
             # target tighter
             target_max = cur_max * (1 - 0.001*min(no,10))
             if target_max > 0 and remaining > 80:
                 b2 = solve_response_lp(parent, target_max, min(60, remaining*0.35))
                 if b2 is not None:
                    b2sum=float(np.sum(b2))
                    if b2sum > 1e-12:
                       g2=np.clip(b2,0,10)/b2sum
                       for t in [1,0.5,0.2]: evaluate and update local_best/global
             if local_best_sc < parent_score -1e-10:
                 parent=local_best; parent_score=local_best_sc; no=0
             else:
                 if no >= 6:
                    # local
                    local_budget = min(20.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.2)
                    if local_budget > 5 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 35:
                        a2,sc2 = diagonal_descent(parent, local_budget, rng)
                        if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < parent_score:
                           parent=a2; parent_score=sc2; best_seq,best_score=consider_array(parent,...); no=0
                    no += 3 # force break soon
          if no >= 10:
              break
    return
```

def run_parametric_length(n, rng, best_seq, best_score, deadline):
    if n <=0: return
    n=int(n)
    if n ==1:
       best_seq,best_score=consider_array(np.ones(1),...)
       return
    xfrac = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    base = make_binomial(n,0.5)
    best_seq,best_score=consider_array(base,...)
    for alpha in (...):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       arr=make_binomial(n,alpha)
       best_seq,best_score=consider_array(arr,...)
    # powers
    idx=np.arange(n)
    for shift in (...):
       if time... break
       arr=1.0/np.sqrt(idx+shift)
       consider
    # gamma
    for gamma in (...):
       arr=np.power(base, gamma)
       consider
    # reverse
    rev=base[::-1]
    for beta in (...):
       arr=base + beta*rev
       consider
    # bump additive
    for amp in (...):
       for mu in (...):
          for sig in (...):
             gauss=np.exp(-(((xfrac-mu)/sig)**2))
             arr=base + amp*gauss
             consider
             if time > deadline -2: break all? use flag
    # linear factor
    for t in (...):
       arr=base*(1.0+t*xfrac)
       if np.min(arr) > -1e-9: consider
       arr=base*(1.0+t*(1-xfrac))
       if min> -1e-9: consider
    return

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step-function heights.

Algorithm summary:
- Use the square-root-of-geometric-family sequences (partial coefficients of
  (1-x)^(-1/2)) as strong initializations, plus several smooth parametric
  variants and several lengths.
- Run a repeated linear-program best-response iteration inspired by the
  previous state of the art: given f, solve the LP maximizing sum(g)
  subject to f*g <= max(f*f), then blend/update.
- Polish the best discovered candidates with stochastic diagonal multiplicative
  steps and random block perturbations.
"""

import time
import math
import gc

import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
    import scipy.optimize as opt
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def normalize_array(a):
    """Return a nonnegative, finite, length-normalized np array or None."""
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_conv(a, b=None):
    """Full convolution. Uses direct for moderate sizes and FFT for large ones."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None:
        b = a
    else:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    na = a.size
    nb = b.size
    if na == 0 or nb == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if max(na, nb) <= 3000:
        return np.convolve(a, b)

    need = na + nb - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need:
        nfft <<= 1

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    if b is a:
        fb = fa
        use_sq = True
    else:
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
        use_sq = False

    if use_sq:
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)[:need]
    else:
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, nfft)[:need]

    # Remove tiny negative roundoff; all true values are nonnegative.
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def sequence_score_fast(a):
    """Fast local evaluation matching the normalization-invariant score."""
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = a.size
    c = fast_conv(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb)


def consider_array(a, best_seq, best_score):
    """Update (best_seq,best_score) if a has a lower fast score."""
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = sequence_score_fast(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
        return a, sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def make_binomial(n, alpha=0.5):
    """
    Coefficients proportional to those of (1-x)^(-alpha).
    For alpha=1/2 this is the classical sqrt-of-geometric sequence.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if alpha <= 1e-14:
        out[0] = 1.0
        out[1:] = 0.0
        return out

    v = 1.0
    for i in range(n):
        out[i] = v
        if i + 1 < n:
            v *= (float(i) + float(alpha)) / float(i + 1)
            if v <= 0.0:
                out[i + 1:] = 0.0
                break
    return out


def run_parametric_length(n, rng, best_seq, best_score, deadline):
    """Evaluate a variety of cheap parametric candidates of length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    if n == 1:
        return consider_array(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), best_seq, best_score)

    xfrac = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    base = make_binomial(n, 0.5)
    best_seq, best_score = consider_array(base, best_seq, best_score)

    # Different analytic powers.
    for alpha in (0.4, 0.45, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = make_binomial(n, alpha)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)

    # Simple reciprocal-square-root shifts.
    for shift in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = 1.0 / np.sqrt(idx + shift)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)

    # Gamma warps of the basic sqrt-geometric sequence.
    for gamma in (0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = np.power(base, gamma)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)

    # Add reversed mass, controlled by beta.
    rev = base[::-1]
    for beta in (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = base + float(beta) * rev
        best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)

    # Add Gaussian late-end bumps.
    stop = False
    for amp in (0.005, 0.02, 0.08, 0.3, 1.0, 4.0):
        if stop or time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        for mu in (0.65, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00):
            if stop or time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            for sig in (0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.45):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                    stop = True
                    break
                g = np.exp(-(((xfrac - mu) / sig) ** 2))
                arr = base + amp * g
                best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)

    # Linear tapers.
    for t in (-0.6, -0.3, -0.1, 0.05, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = base * (1.0 + t * xfrac)
        if np.min(arr) > -1e-9:
            best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)
        arr = base * (1.0 - 0.5 * xfrac + t * (xfrac * xfrac))
        if np.min(arr) > -1e-9:
            best_seq, best_score = consider_array(arr, best_seq, best_score)

    return best_seq, best_score


def diag_multiplicative_step(a, k, eta):
    """
    Multiplicative update affecting one convolution diagonal k.
    Reduces the selected diagonal while preserving nonnegativity.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or k < 0 or k >= 2 * n - 1:
        return a

    lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
    hi = min(n - 1, k)
    if lo > hi:
        return a

    i = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    d = k - i
    factor = a[i] + a[d]

    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga[i] = loga[i] - float(eta) * factor

    loga = loga - np.max(loga)
    a2 = np.exp(loga)
    s = float(a2.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return a
    a2 /= s
    return a2


def diagonal_descent(a, budget_s, rng):
    """
    Stochastic multiplicative diagonal descent.
    Returns (best sequence, best fast score).
    """
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    original = a.copy()
    best = a.copy()
    sc = sequence_score_fast(best)
    best_sc = sc

    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return best, best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s
    eta = 0.05
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        h = fast_conv(a, a)
        if h.size == 0:
            break
        maxh = float(h.max())
        minh = float(h.min())
        if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 0.0:
            break

        threshold = maxh - 0.10 * (maxh - minh)
        active = np.where(h >= threshold)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(h))], dtype=np.int64)

        if active.size > 200:
            active = active[rng.choice(active.size, 200, replace=False)]
        k = int(active[rng.integers(active.size)])

        a2 = diag_multiplicative_step(a, k, eta)
        sc2 = sequence_score_fast(a2)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-12:
            a = a2
            sc = sc2
            no_improve = 0
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
            eta = min(0.25, eta * 1.02)
        else:
            # Occasionally accept a small hill-climb move to escape stagnation.
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc * (1.0 + 0.004) and rng.random() < 0.25:
                a = a2
                sc = sc2
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 30:
                    eta *= 0.75
                    no_improve = 0
                    if eta < 1e-5:
                        break

    if best_sc > sc:
        best = a.copy()
        best_sc = sc
    if best_sc > sequence_score_fast(original):
        best = original
        best_sc = sequence_score_fast(best)

    return best, best_sc


def random_block_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    """Random multiplicative block perturbation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()

    c = a.copy()
    nblocks = 1
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        nblocks = 2

    for _ in range(nblocks):
        wmin = max(2, n // 300)
        wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 15)
        w = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax))
        w = min(w, n)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        mult = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma))))
        c[start:start + w] *= mult

        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            w2 = int(rng.integers(max(1, w // 3), w + 1))
            w2 = min(w2, w)
            mult2 = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma))))
            c[start:start + w2] *= mult2

    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize_array(c)


def local_block_search(a, budget_s, rng):
    """Random-block local search with annealing-like step control."""
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = a.copy()
    sc = sequence_score_fast(best)
    best_sc = sc

    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return best, best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s
    sigma = 0.15
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = random_block_perturb(best, rng, sigma)
        if cand is None:
            break
        sc2 = sequence_score_fast(cand)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc * (1.0 + 0.0015):
            if sc2 < sc or rng.random() < 0.20:
                best = cand
                sc = sc2
                no_improve = 0
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_sc = sc2
                sigma = min(0.30, sigma * 1.05)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 70:
                sigma *= 0.78
                no_improve = 0
                if sigma < 1e-3:
                    break

    if best_sc > sc:
        best = best.copy()
        best_sc = sc
    if best_sc > sequence_score_fast(a):
        best = a
        best_sc = sequence_score_fast(a)

    return best, best_sc


def build_response_matrix(vals):
    """
    Build A for full convolution constraints A g <= 1,
    where A[k,j] = vals[k-j] if 0 <= k-j < n.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    n = int(vals.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None

    try:
        # D[i, :] is the diagonal for offset -i, with constant value vals[i].
        D = np.empty((n, n), dtype=np.float64)
        D[:, :] = vals
        offsets = np.arange(0, -n, -1, dtype=np.int32)
        A = sp.diags(D, offsets=offsets, shape=(2 * n - 1, n), format="csr")
        del D
        return A
    except Exception:
        # Fallback: explicit COO construction (heavier but robust).
        try:
            cols = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
            rows_parts = []
            cols_parts = []
            data_parts = []
            for d in range(n):
                rows_parts.append((cols + d).astype(np.int32, copy=False))
                cols_parts.append(cols)
                data_parts.append(np.full(n, float(vals[d]), dtype=np.float64))
            rows = np.concatenate(rows_parts)
            cols_all = np.concatenate(cols_parts)
            data = np.concatenate(data_parts)
            A = sp.coo_matrix(
                (data, (rows, cols_all)),
                shape=(2 * n - 1, n)
            ).tocsr()
            return A
        except Exception:
            return None


def solve_response_lp(a, rhs, timeout_s=60.0):
    """
    Solve max sum(g) subject to (a*g)_k <= rhs, 0 <= g_i <= 10.
    Returns g or None.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None

    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 16 or n > 3200:
        return None

    rhs = float(rhs)
    if not np.isfinite(rhs) or rhs <= 0.0:
        return None

    vals = a / rhs
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        return None

    A = build_response_matrix(vals)
    if A is None:
        return None

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = (0.0, 10.0)

    timeout_s = max(1.0, float(timeout_s))

    rc = None
    try:
        rc = opt.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": timeout_s}
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            rc = opt.linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs"
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if rc is None:
        return None

    x = getattr(rc, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n:
        return None

    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 10.0)

    # A often remains in memory after function return, but help GC for long runs.
    del A
    if int(x.size) % 37 == 0:
        gc.collect()

    return x


def run_lp_chain(parent, parent_score, rng, deadline, lp_n, best_seq, best_score):
    """
    Run the LP best-response chain on a fixed length.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best_seq, best_score, parent, parent_score

    n = int(lp_n)
    if n <= 0:
        return best_seq, best_score, parent, parent_score

    parent = normalize_array(parent)
    if parent is None:
        return best_seq, best_score, None, float("inf")

    parent_score = sequence_score_fast(parent)
    best_seq, best_score = consider_array(parent, best_seq, best_score)

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    max_iters = 200

    while it < max_iters:
        it += 1

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 25.0:
            break

        h = fast_conv(parent, parent)
        cur_max = float(h.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cur_max) or cur_max <= 0.0:
            break

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        timeout = min(120.0, remaining * 0.55)
        if timeout < 20.0:
            break

        rhs = cur_max * (1.0 + 1e-8)
        b = solve_response_lp(parent, rhs, timeout)
        if b is None:
            break

        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
        b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 10.0)
        bsum = float(b.sum())
        if bsum <= 1e-12:
            break

        g = b / bsum
        local_best = parent
        local_best_sc = parent_score

        for t in (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.1, 0.03):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * parent + t * g
            cand = normalize_array(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = sequence_score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best = cand
                local_best_sc = sc
            best_seq, best_score = consider_array(cand, best_seq, best_score)

        if local_best_sc < parent_score - 1e-10:
            parent = local_best
            parent_score = local_best_sc
            no_improve = 0
            if it % 2 == 0:
                print(f"LP chain n={n} iter={it}: parent score={parent_score:.9f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if it % 5 == 0:
                print(f"LP chain n={n} iter={it}: no improvement, bsum={bsum:.4f}")

            # Try a slightly tighter right-hand side to encourage descent.
            if no_improve >= 2:
                target_max = cur_max * (1.0 - 0.0012 * min(no_improve, 12.0))
                if target_max > 0.0 and target_max < rhs and remaining > 90.0:
                    b2 = solve_response_lp(parent, target_max, min(60.0, remaining * 0.35))
                    if b2 is not None:
                        b2 = np.asarray(b2, dtype=np.float64)
                        b2 = np.where(np.isfinite(b2), b2, 0.0)
                        b2 = np.clip(b2, 0.0, 10.0)
                        b2sum = float(b2.sum())
                        if b2sum > 1e-12:
                            g2 = b2 / b2sum
                            for t in (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05):
                                cand = (1.0 - t) * parent + t * g2
                                cand = normalize_array(cand)
                                if cand is None:
                                    continue
                                sc = sequence_score_fast(cand)
                                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
                                    local_best = cand
                                    local_best_sc = sc
                                best_seq, best_score = consider_array(cand, best_seq, best_score)

            if local_best_sc < parent_score - 1e-10:
                parent = local_best
                parent_score = local_best_sc
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                # Perturb/search if the LP is stuck.
                if no_improve >= 6:
                    local_budget = min(20.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20)
                    if local_budget > 5.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
                        a2, sc2 = diagonal_descent(parent, local_budget, rng)
                        if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < parent_score:
                            parent = a2
                            parent_score = sc2
                            best_seq, best_score = consider_array(parent, best_seq, best_score)
                            no_improve = 0
                    no_improve += 3

            if no_improve >= 12:
                break

    return best_seq, best_score, parent, parent_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total_budget = 1800.0
    try:
        if budget_s is not None and float(budget_s) > 0.0:
            total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1800.0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    margin = 12.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, total_budget - margin)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Use previously found construction if present.
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1  # assumed global, not imported
        best_seq, best_score = consider_array(h, best_seq, best_score)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Reserve time. If SciPy is available, leave enough for LP chains.
    if HAVE_SCIPY:
        param_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + min(300.0, max(60.0, total_budget * 0.22)))
    else:
        param_deadline = deadline - 120.0

    lengths = [2535, 2048, 4096, 1024, 8192, 1536, 512]
    for N in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > param_deadline - 4.0:
            break
        if N > 10000:
            continue
        best_seq, best_score = run_parametric_length(N, rng, best_seq, best_score, param_deadline)
        if best_score < float("inf"):
            print(f"Parametric len={N}: best_score={best_score:.9f}")

    # LP chains.
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        n1 = 2535
        parent = normalize_array(make_binomial(n1, 0.5))
        if parent is not None and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n1:
            sc_best = sequence_score_fast(best_seq)
            sc_parent = sequence_score_fast(parent)
            if sc_best < sc_parent:
                parent = best_seq

        # Short pre-polish, then LP.
        if parent is not None:
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0:
                child, sc_child = diagonal_descent(parent, 20.0, rng)
                if child is not None and np.isfinite(sc_child):
                    best_seq, best_score = consider_array(child, best_seq, best_score)
                    if child is not None:
                        parent = child
            parent_score = sequence_score_fast(parent)

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            first_lp_deadline = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, rem * 0.55))
            best_seq, best_score, parent, parent_score = run_lp_chain(
                parent,
                parent_score,
                rng,
                first_lp_deadline,
                n1,
                best_seq,
                best_score
            )

        # Second LP chain at a smaller length if time remains.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
            n2 = 1024
            parent2 = normalize_array(make_binomial(n2, 0.5))
            if parent2 is not None and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n2:
                sc_best = sequence_score_fast(best_seq)
                sc_parent2 = sequence_score_fast(parent2)
                if sc_best < sc_parent2:
                    parent2 = best_seq

            if parent2 is not None:
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
                    child2, sc_child2 = diagonal_descent(parent2, 10.0, rng)
                    if child2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc_child2):
                        best_seq, best_score = consider_array(child2, best_seq, best_score)
                        parent2 = child2
                parent2_score = sequence_score_fast(parent2)

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                second_lp_deadline = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, rem * 0.40))
                best_seq, best_score, parent2, parent2_score = run_lp_chain(
                    parent2,
                    parent2_score,
                    rng,
                    second_lp_deadline,
                    n2,
                    best_seq,
                    best_score
                )

    # Final polishing of the global best.
    if best_seq is not None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 25.0:
            budget = min(180.0, max(5.0, rem * 0.35))
            a2, sc2 = diagonal_descent(best_seq, budget, rng)
            if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_seq, best_score = a2, sc2

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 15.0:
            budget = min(90.0, max(5.0, rem * 0.45))
            a2, sc2 = local_block_search(best_seq, budget, rng)
            if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_seq, best_score = a2, sc2

    # Fallback if everything failed.
    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_seq)):
        best_seq = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = sequence_score_fast(best_seq)

    # Final sanitization: finite, nonnegative, not all zero.
    arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    # Keep the sum comfortably above the evaluator's zero-sum guard.
    if 0.0 < s < 0.01:
        arr = arr / s

    # One last optional local tweak if extreme time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0 and arr.size > 0:
        a2, sc2 = diagonal_descent(arr, min(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0), rng)
        if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            arr = np.asarray(a2, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"Final: length={arr.size}, fast_score={best_score:.9f}, elapsed={elapsed:.2f}s")

    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```
